This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and what it asks and predicts is shaped by them. **Follow-up 10's result is the one that matters**: `rd` (a run of three far, a first reading near, the plain lock) reached 18 of 180 under range-law false returns, 36 under the uniform false rate, 99 under the persistent mix, 130 under dropouts and **152 on a sea with no faults against the clean map's 170**, and follow-up 17's set measured `rd` at 14 of 180 under range-law false returns with dropouts. The mechanism its note gives (a near false return is marked at the first reading and held thirty readings) does not depend on the far mark, so **this round is registered expecting the plain lock to be poor whatever its gate**, and asks only whether the ramp (24 readings at 40 m, 18 at 80 m, against the step's 30) or the nearest range moves it. Follow-up 19's set (the same lock with the step, `rm23lk`) is unread or unflown. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The question.** A plain lock holds every marked cell for the whole of its gate whatever the cell's history, so a near false return is held as long as a wall. The evidence hold (follow-up 12) was made to fix that and does. The ramp shortens the lock near the vessel and lengthens it far from it; the nearest range ever read keeps a wall held after the vessel has passed it. Neither touches the problem, which is the lock's length for a cell read once. The round asks whether the shape or the range changes the size of a collapse, not whether a lock can win.

**The rule** (follow-up 19's detector with the gate chosen). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading. A marked cell is cleared after `g(r)` consecutive free readings, `g` the **step** (30 if `r` is at most 80 m, else 1) or the **ramp** (`max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`), `r` the range **at the ping** (`ping`) or the **least range the cell has been read occupied from since it was last cleared** (`min`).

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 19's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 35): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rm23re30`** (follow-up 17's designated: the same mark with the evidence hold, here the control that does not collapse), **`rm23lk`** (follow-up 19's designated: step, `ping`), **`rm23lkmn`** (step, `min`), **`rm23lkrp`** (ramp, `ping`) and **`rm23lkrn`** (**the designated: ramp, `min`**, as follow-up 15's); `inc`, `rm23lk` and `rm23lkrn` under `W` (3) and under `P` (3); `rm23lkrn` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD41 = seeds 4101 to 4160 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm23lkrn` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 19's bar. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The ramp is the one gate that could matter: at 40 m it holds 24 readings where the step holds 30, at 60 m 21, at 80 m 18, so the near phantoms are held a fifth to two-fifths less; and it holds a far cell (12 at 120 m) where the step lets it go at once, which is the thing follow-up 15 found carries the narrow gaps. Against it: a lock of 18 to 24 readings is still a lock, and follow-up 10's collapse has no threshold in it.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AK).**
* **AK1** the near phantom problem remains: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 30 fewer than `rm23re30`. (Follow-up 17: `rm23re30` 118, `rd` 14.)
* **AK2** which range and shape the lock goes by does not rescue it: under `R3D30`, the four lock arms (`rm23lk`, `rm23lkmn`, `rm23lkrp`, `rm23lkrn`) have pooled counts within 20 of each other (largest minus smallest). (All four hold a near false return for 18 to 30 readings.)
* **AK3** a lock is worse than the map that forgets: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 20 fewer than `inc`.
* **AK4** `rm23lkrn` does not hold the bar: under `R3D30`, it is below 80% of exact in each of the three kinds.
* **AK5** where the law fails it is worse: under `W`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 20 fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@W` 36 against `inc` 123.)
* **AK6** under the persistent mix `P`, `rm23lkrn` reaches fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@P` 99 against 148.)
* **AK7** the ramp shortens the damage a little: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches more than `rm23lk`. (18 to 24 readings within 80 m against thirty, over the first 80 m.)
* **AK8** a lock costs even on a sea with no faults: `rm23lkrn@clean` reaches at least 8 fewer than the clean `long+fix+forget`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@clean` 152 against 170: the fix's flicker is held.)
`rm23lk` against `rm23lkrn` per kind and per gap, and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan (the phantom count) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 200 m, 30 and 2-of-3 are the right numbers; that the ramp and the step are the only gates (a gate that falls faster near the vessel is untried); that a plain lock cannot be made to work (a lock that is short for a cell read once is the evidence hold, which is follow-ups 12 to 17); that the range law is true of a real sonar. The round is expected to confirm a collapse, and says so before the data. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`rm23lkmn`, `rm23lkrp` and `rm23lkrn`; `rm23lk`, `rm23re30` and `inc` exist), the set HELD41 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD41 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 37: THE SECOND HOLD THAT KNOWS HOW FAR (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 32's name (`detector-hold-range-hold`: follow-up 15's ramp hold with the carried evidence, the second hold) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 17 made on follow-up 16, follow-up 31 on follow-up 24 and follow-up 33 on follow-up 13: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 32's carry restores a cleared cell's evidence (to 4) when the cell is next read occupied within 60 readings, at every range. Here the carry is **judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell**: **only beyond 80 m** (`far`), **only within it** (`near`), or at every range (`all`: follow-up 32's). The reason a range can matter to a carry is the ramp: the hold `min(ev, g(r))` is 12 readings at 120 m, 6 at 160 m and 4 at 175 m, so a far cell that a dropout clears has little to lose, and restoring its evidence to 4 gains most there; a near cell was held up to 30 readings and cleared after that many free ones, which looks like a real clear, and a near persistent phantom is the one the planner cannot afford. If it meant the carry's *amount* depending on range, or the range of follow-up 15's gate varied again, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp carries; `rn30` held the bar), follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 17's (a first-reading mark within 80 m is where the harm is done). Follow-up 32, which this one rests on, is **registered and unbuilt**: its carry is a field the module does not have yet and its prediction (AG1, the carry changes little under the dropout) is made without any result. The sets of follow-ups 19 and 21 to 24 are unread or unflown. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The question, and what to expect.** If follow-up 32's carry changes little under the dropout, as that round registered, then restricting it to a zone changes little too, and this round measures a small effect more finely; it is registered with that said. What it can show that follow-up 32 cannot is whether the effect, if there is one, lives in the far zone (where the hold is short) or the near zone (where the phantoms are).

**The rule** (follow-up 32's detector with a zone). Mark: one occupied reading. Clear: after `thr = min(ev, g(r))` consecutive free readings, `ev` the occupied readings since last cleared, `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`, `r` the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from (`min`). **Carry with a zone**: a cell cleared with `ev` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from beyond 80 m (`far`), within 80 m inclusive (`near`) or from any range (`all`)** it is marked with `ev = 4`; a ghost read occupied from the other zone is marked as an ordinary cell (`ev` 1). After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 36): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-up 15's and 32's; the bar's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a carried phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one and the ramp hold on the nearest range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rn30`** (follow-up 15's designated, no carry), **`rn30c`** (follow-up 32's designated: the carry at every range), **`rn30cf`** (**the designated: the carry beyond 80 m only**) and **`rn30cn`** (the carry within 80 m only); under `WD30L` `rn30`, `rn30c` and `rn30cf` (3); under `P` `inc`, `rn30`, `rn30c` and `rn30cf` (4); `rn30cf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD42 = seeds 4201 to 4260 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30cf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30`**: three cells, follow-up 15's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30L`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar; `rn30` held this bar in follow-up 15, so a verdict here speaks of the carry only through the predictions.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far carry restores a cell to a hold of 4, and the ramp's hold at 160 m is 6: the restoration can only matter between about 80 m and 175 m, a band in which a dropout of 30 pings has to start and end within the ghost's life for the carry to act at all; the near carry restores a cell that was held up to 30 readings to 4, shorter than what it had. Both effects are small by construction. The persistent mix returns the same false returns, and a far one carried is a phantom the planner sees from a distance for 4 readings.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AL).**
* **AL1** the far carry does what the carry at every range does: under `D30`, `rn30cf` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (The far band is where the ramp's hold is short.)
* **AL2** and the near carry does nothing: under `D30`, `rn30cn` is within 8 of `rn30` (either side). (A near cell is held for what it earned.)
* **AL3** the far carry is cheaper under the persistent mix: under `P`, `rn30cf` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30c`. (The persistent false returns that matter are near.)
* **AL4** under the leak the zone does not matter: under `WD30L`, `rn30cf` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG2.)
* **AL5** `rn30cf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **AL6** the designated beats the map that forgets under the dropout: under `D30`, `rn30cf` reaches more than `inc`. (Follow-up 15's T4 asked 10 more and missed at 4 more.)
* **AL7** under the persistent mix `P`, `rn30cf` is within 10 of `rn30` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG3, asked of the far carry.)
* **AL8** on a sea with no faults `rn30cf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30cf` against `rn30c` per kind and per gap (the 24 m gaps especially) and the number of carried re-marks by zone (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the re-marking ping is the right range for the carry (the range at the clearing ping, or the nearest the cell was read from, are untried); that the dropouts are like a real sonar's (they are made up, and so is the leak). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, follow-up 32's `ghost.carry` and a zone for it (`ghost.zone`: `all` by default, `far` or `near`; validation, the mark path, `describe`, gate rows and mutants), two registry rows (`rn30cf` and `rn30cn`; `rn30`, `rn30c` and `inc` exist after follow-up 32's), the set HELD42 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD42 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 38: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, ON THE CONFIRMED GATED HOLD (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 29's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm-hold`: the second hold, the ghost, on the confirmed hold, gated and ungated) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 37 made on follow-up 32 and follow-up 17 on follow-up 16: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 29's ghost re-marks a cleared cell (one that had at least 4 occupied readings) at **one** occupied reading, whatever the mark rule, at every range, for 60 readings of that cell. Here the ghost is **judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell**: **only beyond 80 m** (`far`), **only within it** (`near`), or at every range (`all`: follow-up 29's). The hold under it is the name's `range-hold`: the **gated** confirmed hold (follow-up 24: the evidence hold of cap 30 within 80 m and one free reading beyond, cleared by 0.8 of the window). The reason a range can matter is the gate itself: **beyond 80 m the gated hold lets a dropout take a cell at once**, so the far zone is where there is something to restore at one reading, and it is also where the range law puts the most false returns (0.06 at 200 m against 0.0054 at 60 m), so the far zone is where a restored cell is least safe. If it meant the confirmed clear's fraction depending on range (follow-up 31's) with the ghost, or the zone judged by the range at the clearing ping, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; removing the gate paid +10; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 17's (a first-reading mark within 80 m is where the harm is done). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the confirmed gated hold (follow-up 24) and the ghost behind the confirmed hold (follow-up 29) are unread or unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without their results. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 29's detector with a zone on the ghost). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell is **marked** when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g, occ)` readings were free, `g` = 30 for a cell **within 80 m** of the sonar at the ping and 1 **beyond** it (the gated hold). A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from beyond 80 m (`far`), within 80 m inclusive (`near`) or from any range (`all`)** it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; read occupied from the other zone it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 29's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 37): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the false rate 0.01 uniform, dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's designated: the gated confirmed hold, no ghost), **`m23re30kg`** (follow-up 29's: the ghost at every range), **`m23re30kgf`** (**the designated: the ghost beyond 80 m only**), **`m23re30kgn`** (the ghost within 80 m only) and **`m23e30kg`** (follow-up 29's designated: the confirmed hold at every range with the ghost); under `WD30L` `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` (3); under `P` `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` (3); `m23re30kgf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD43 = seeds 4301 to 4360 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23re30kgf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far ghost is the one stage that answers the gate's cost (a dropout that begins far away takes the cell at once; the ghost brings it back at one reading) and the one that meets the range law's worst false returns; the near ghost restores a cell that was held up to 30 readings and is safe under the law and not under the uniform rate. A ghost that re-marks at one reading a far cell once confirmed is as good as the cell's evidence, which for a far false return that passed a 2-of-3 and four readings is small.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AM).**
* **AM1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side). (The gate is where there is something to restore.)
* **AM2** and the near ghost does nothing: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgn` is within 8 of `m23re30k` (either side). (A near cell is held for what it earned.)
* **AM3** the far ghost helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgf` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3, +9, asked of the far zone.)
* **AM4** under the leak the zone does not matter: under `WD30L`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side).
* **AM5** `m23re30kgf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AM6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AM7** the far ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30k` (either side). (Follow-up 18: `rm23e30g@P` 159 against `inc` 160.)
* **AM8** on a sea with no faults `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23re30kgf` against `m23e30kg` under `R3D30` (does the far ghost make the gate unnecessary: follow-up 18's J1 and J4), the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks by zone (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the re-marking ping is the right range for the ghost; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, a zone for the ghost (`ghost.zone`: `all` by default, `far` or `near`; validation, the re-marking path, `describe`, gate rows and mutants; the same field follow-up 37 needs, built once), two registry rows (`m23re30kgf` and `m23re30kgn`; `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23e30kg` are follow-ups 24 and 29's), the set HELD43 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD43 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 39: WHICH RANGE-AWARE CONFIRMATION WHEN THE CLEAR IS CONFIRMED TOO (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 26's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range`: which `M`-of-`N` mark beyond 80 m, one reading within, when the evidence hold is there) with `-confirm` on the end, the move follow-up 25 made on follow-up 19, follow-up 24 on follow-up 13 and follow-up 27 on follow-up 15. The confirmation after the hold is the hold's, as in each of those: **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings free). Taken as **follow-up 22's question asked of the range-aware mark**, as follow-up 26 asked follow-up 20's: with the evidence hold at every range and its clear confirmed, which rule should the mark use beyond 80 m, with one reading within it? Follow-up 26 ends by saying that "a confirmed clear (follow-up 21) ... changes the order of the rules" is untested; this is that test. If it meant the confirmed clear's fraction depending on range (follow-up 31's) or the whole range-aware detector with the clear confirmed (follow-up 25's), say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 20's (under a hold the five confirming rules are one rule, spread 6 of 180; strictness cost where the misses are many), follow-up 17's and 18's (the range-aware mark with a hold was below the uniform pair when the hold was gated, level with it when the hold was at every range: `rm23e30` 134, `m23e30` 135 under R3D30; a first-reading mark within 80 m costs under the uniform false rate). **The confirmed clear itself (follow-up 21) and the family under it (follow-up 22) are unread or unflown**, and follow-up 22 registered the opposite of the first prediction below (that the family does *not* collapse under a confirmed clear) before any result; this one is made with follow-up 20 in view. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 26's detector with the clear confirmed). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, at every range. With the mark of one at every range it is follow-up 21's `m11f80`.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 38): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law) and **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, the law NOT holding, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the confirmed clear's price is), and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range and the clear of `f80`): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the seven detectors **`m11f80`** (a mark of one everywhere: the control that repairs nothing), **`rm22f80`**, **`rm33f80`**, **`rm23f80`** and **`rm34f80`** (**the designated: 3-of-4 beyond 80 m, one reading within**), and, for the comparison with the same rule everywhere, **`m23f80`** (follow-up 21's designated) and **`m34f80`** (follow-up 22's); under `WD30L` `m11f80`, `rm23f80`, `rm34f80`, `m23f80` and `m34f80` (5); and `rm34f80` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD44 = seeds 4401 to 4460 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34f80` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (The clean sea is in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The two registered effects of the confirmed clear (it cleans the fix smear that a long hold thickens; it clears a leaking dropout sooner) are felt in both zones, and the far zone is where the range law puts the false returns and the strict rule is cheap. A strict far rule is slow to re-mark a cell the clear takes (follow-up 22's X2); the near zone, marked at the first reading, takes a near false return at once, which `WD30L` (the law NOT holding) is built to find, with the leak on top.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AN).**
* **AN1** the family collapses under a confirmed clear too: under `R3D30`, the four range-aware confirming rules (`rm22f80`, `rm33f80`, `rm23f80`, `rm34f80`) have pooled counts within 20 of each other (largest minus smallest). (Follow-up 20's Y1 with the near zone alike in all four.)
* **AN2** every far confirmation repairs the range-law false returns: under `R3D30`, each of the four reaches at least 15 more than `m11f80`. (Follow-up 26's AA2.)
* **AN3** with the hold at every range the range-aware mark is level with the uniform one: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80` is within 8 of `m34f80` (either side). (`rm23e30` 134 and `m23e30` 135 in follow-up 18.)
* **AN4** strict and light are one rule under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80` is within 8 of `rm23f80` (either side). (Follow-up 20's Y1.)
* **AN5** `rm34f80` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AN6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AN7** the bet on the law has a price: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80` reaches at least 10 fewer than `m34f80`. (Follow-up 26's AA6; follow-up 17's Q5.)
* **AN8** on a sea with no faults `rm34f80` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34f80` against `rm23f80` per kind and per gap, `m23f80` and `m34f80` against the range-aware rules under `WD30L`, and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 3-of-4, 0.8 and a cap of 30 are the right numbers or that the far rule should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the readings of a cell are independent; that the range law is true of a real sonar; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that a gated hold would not change the order. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`rm22f80`, `rm33f80` and `rm34f80`; `rm23f80` is follow-up 25's row and `m11f80`, `m23f80` and `m34f80` are follow-ups 21 and 22's), the set HELD44 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD44 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 40: THE GHOST BEHIND THE CONFIRMED CLEAR THAT KNOWS HOW FAR (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-range-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 31's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm-range`: the confirmed clear whose fraction depends on range) with `-hold` on the end, the move follow-ups 18, 29, 30, 32, 34 and 35 made: **a second hold, on what has been held**, which is **the ghost** (a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time it is marked again at that reading, with its evidence put back to 4; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). Follow-up 31's detector is kept as it was (the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range; a marked cell cleared when at least `K = ceil(f L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, `f` = `fNear` for a cell within 80 m at the ping and `fFar` beyond). The reason the two belong together is the one follow-up 31 gives: **the confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner** (follow-up 21's A4), and the ghost is the stage that puts such a cell back at one reading; what is new is that the clear's fraction can now differ by range, so what the ghost restores differs by range too. If it meant the ghost judged by range (that is follow-up 38's) or the hold range-gated, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, and its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule; strictness cost where the misses are many). **The confirmed clear itself (follow-up 21), the ghost behind the confirmed hold (follow-up 29) and the fraction by range (follow-up 31) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without any result: the claim that it clears a leaking dropout sooner is a registered mechanism, not a finding. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 31's detector with a switch). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell is **marked** when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, `f` = `fNear` (within 80 m of the sonar at the ping, inclusive) or `fFar` (beyond). **With the ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; after 60 it is forgotten. With `fNear` = `fFar` and no ghost it is follow-up 21's detector.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 39): **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak is), **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the range law with the false rate 0.03 at its mean and dropouts of 30 pings, no leak: where the smear is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the 2-of-3 mark and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `WD30L` and `R3D30` the five detectors **`f80`** (0.8 at every range: follow-up 21's designated), **`fn100f80`** (a run within 80 m, 0.8 beyond: follow-up 31's designated), **`f80g`** (`f80` with the ghost), **`fn100f80g`** (**the designated: the same with the ghost**) and **`fn80f100g`** (0.8 within, a run beyond, with the ghost: the opposite bet) (10 arms); under `P` `fn100f80` and `fn100f80g` (2); and `fn100f80g` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD45 = seeds 4501 to 4560 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `fn100f80g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30L` and under `R3D30`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** A strict fraction near and a tolerant one far is follow-up 31's bet: the leak is paid near and the smear is cheap far. The ghost cuts across it. It restores a cell the tolerant far clear took, at one reading, so it makes the far tolerance cheaper still; and it restores a smear edge cell that a strict near clear would have kept, which is the cure follow-up 21 registered and the ghost undoes. Under the persistent mix the ghost restores a persistent false return that once earned four readings. The map that forgets is not an arm here (the arms are compared with each other and with `exact`).

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AO).**
* **AO1** the ghost answers the leak behind the strict near clear: under `WD30L`, `fn100f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `fn100f80`. (A leaking dropout clears cells far away; the ghost restores them at one reading. Follow-up 29's AD4.)
* **AO2** and helps under dropouts without a leak: under `R3D30`, `fn100f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `fn100f80`. (Follow-up 18's J3 and J4: +9 and +5.)
* **AO3** with a ghost the fraction by range is one rule: under `WD30L`, `fn100f80g` is within 8 of `f80g` (either side). (The ghost restores what the clear takes.)
* **AO4** and the opposite bet is no better: under `R3D30`, `fn80f100g` is within 8 of `fn100f80g` (either side).
* **AO5** `fn100f80g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AO6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AO7** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `fn100f80g` is within 8 of `fn100f80` (either side). (Follow-up 18: `rm23e30g@P` 159 against `inc` 160.)
* **AO8** on a sea with no faults `fn100f80g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`f80g` against `f80` per sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 30, 1.0, 0.8, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers or that the fraction should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the ghost is the only second hold (a decayed evidence, a hold on the neighbours, a K-of-N clear are untried). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, `clear.fNear` (default `f`; the same field follow-up 31 needs, built once), three registry rows (`f80g`, `fn100f80g` and `fn80f100g`; `f80`/`m23f80` is follow-up 21's, `fn100f80` follow-up 31's, and `f80g` is the detector follow-up 23 calls `gU`), the set HELD45 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD45 is.
